    CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
    主席報告


                                         In FY2024/2025, we observed a significant
                                         shift in consumer behaviour toward greater
                                         price sensitivity. In the coming year, the
                                         indirect impact caused by heightened
                                         geopolitical factors and the direct impact
                                         due to growth slowdown in the categories
                                         we compete are likely to create challenges
                                         for our business in the short term.
                                         For the longer term, however, we remain
                                         confident in our ability to drive gradual
                                         improvements in both revenue and
                                         profitability.
                                         於 二 零 二 四 ╱ 二 零 二 五 財 政 年 度，我 們 觀
                                         察 到 大 眾 的 消 費 模 式 改 變，明 顯 地 對 價 格
                                         日 趨 敏 感。在 未 來 一 年，地 緣 政 治 局 勢 加
                                         劇所造成的間接影響以及我們在各產品類
                                         別 增 長 放 緩 所 造 成 的 直 接 影 響，或 為 業 務
                                         帶來短期挑戰。
                                         然 而 長 遠 而 言，我 們 堅 信 自 身 實 力，致 力
                                         逐步提升收入及盈利能力。



                                                                 Mr. Winston Yau-lai LO

10   Vitasoy International Holdings Ltd. 維他奶國際集團有限公司
          Group Chief Executive Officer’s Report/Business Review
          集 團行政總裁報告╱業務回顧




Group revenue increased by 1% in FY2024/2025. Sales in our               本集團收入於二零二四╱二零二五財政年度增
biggest market, Mainland China, rose by 1% in local currency             加 1%。我們佔比最大的銷售市場中國內地於該
compared with the previous year, thanks to an improvement in             財政年度下半年改善，其銷售額以當地貨幣計
the second half of the fiscal year. Core products under both the         算較去年上升 1%。儘管茶類飲品市場競爭日益
VITASOY and VITA brands achieved concurrent growth in volume             加劇，但維他奶及維他品牌旗下核心產品的銷
and revenue during the year, despite intensified competition in the      量及收入均於年內同步上升。
tea market.

The Hong Kong Operation delivered another year of revenue growth         香港業務繼去年再實現收入增長，升幅達 2%，
of 2%, driven by strong brand equity, widespread availability and the    主要得益於強大品牌價值、龐大市場覆蓋以及
success of our core products and innovation.                             核心產品和創新成果。

Australia and New Zealand recorded revenue growth of 5% in local         澳洲及新西蘭的收入以當地貨幣計算增長 5%。
currency. This was made possible by the successful resolution of         此乃由於我們完滿解決生產端帶來的困難和挑
the manufacturing challenges, which led to improved operational          戰，最終生產力回復市場需求水平而營運表現
performance and thus the ability to fully satisfy the high demand for    得已改善。
the products in our portfolio.

Our ASEAN operations in Singapore and the Philippines remained           於本財政年度，我們繼續專注推動在新加坡及
focused on driving sustainable growth and reducing losses during the     菲律賓的東南亞業務的持續增長及縮減其虧損。
financial year.

Gross profit margin improved to 51.3%, mainly due to lower               毛 利 率 改 善 並 提 升 至 51.3%，主 要 得 益 於 原 材
commodity costs and a more efficient manufacturing footprint. This       料成本下降及更高效的生產佈局。然而，中國
was partially offset, however, by higher trade promotional expenses      內地的貿易推廣開支增加以及不利匯率變動
in Mainland China and the impact of unfavourable foreign exchange        抵銷部分上述毛利率升幅。經營溢利大幅上升

Tax Strategy                                                              稅務策略
When considering tax, the Group gives due consideration to                當考慮稅務時，本集團會適當考慮其企業及社
the importance of its corporate and social responsibilities. More         會責任的重要性。更明確而言，本集團堅持於
specifically, the Group commits to paying taxes in the countries          其創造價值的國家中繳納稅項，並全面遵守所
where it creates value and complying fully with tax laws across           有相關司法管轄區的稅務法例。本集團同時堅
all relevant jurisdictions. The Group also commits to following           持遵循經濟合作與發展組織轉讓定價指引，並
the Organisation for Economic Co-operation and Development                確保集團公司間的交易時常遵從公平原則。本
(OECD) transfer pricing guidelines and to ensuring that the arm’s         集團亦積極支持經合組織就支柱二有關稅基侵
length principle is always observed in transactions between Group         蝕和利潤轉移的國際稅收改革工作。此外，本
companies. The Group also actively supports the OECD international        集團就集團的稅項事務對稅務機關一直保持公
tax reform work on Base Erosion and Profit Shifting (BEPS) on Pillar      開透明，並且披露相關資料讓稅務機關能執行
Two. In addition, the Group commits to being open and transparent         其覆核工作。
with tax authorities about the Group’s tax affairs and to disclosing

transactions directly related to the underlying businesses of the         為達致協同效益、效率及監控的目的，本集團
Group. For synergy, efficiency and control, the Group operates a          為其所有附屬公司實行中央現金及財政管理系
central cash and treasury management system for all subsidiaries.         統。各營運附屬公司一般以當地貨幣進行借貸，
Borrowings are normally taken out in local currencies by the              為當地投資項目提供資金及減低部份外匯風險。
operating subsidiaries to fund their investments and partially mitigate

      董事提名政策                                           procedures for appointment/re-appointment of Directors.

                                                       appointment/re-appointment of Directors, in particular for INED.
                                                       該政策載有委任╱重新委任董事的甄選及提名標準以及程序。獨
                                                       立性是考慮委任╱重新委任董事（尤其是獨立非執行董事）的因
                                                       素之一。

                                                       The R&N Committee would take account of the independence
                                                       factor in accordance with the Policy before making
                                                       recommendations to the Board for approval of the appointment/
                                                       re-appointment of INED.
                                                       薪酬及提名委員會向董事會提交批准委任╱重新委任獨立非執
                                                       行董事的建議前，根據該政策考慮獨立性因素。

Key Features or Mechanisms                       Implementation and Review of Effectiveness
主要特點或機制                                          執行及檢討成效
Directors Conflict of Interest Policy            The Company has policy and procedures in place to avoid any
董事利益衝突政策                                         potential conflict of interests and not to weaken the objectivity
                                                 and integrity of the Board for decisions making. Under the
                                                 Policy, any Director has to declare his/her directorships outside
                                                 the Company, interests in controlling company, public office
                                                 appointment and any other interests that might be considered
                                                 prejudicial to his/her independence and has to declare his/her
                                                 interests in any transaction in which he/she may have interest and
                                                 abstain from voting on such transaction.
                                                 本公司已制定政策及程序以避免任何潛在利益衝突，且不會削弱
                                                 董事會決策的客觀性及誠信性。根據該政策，任何一位董事必須
                                                 申報其於本公司以外的董事職務、於控股公司中的權益、公職任
                                                 命及任何其他可能被認為有礙其獨立性的利益，並須申報其於可
                                                 能擁有利益的任何交易中的權益，並就該交易放棄投票。

Open Board Culture                               INEDs in family businesses will generally play a more active role in
開明的董事會文化                                         corporate governance area. Our Board culture under the leadership
                                                 of Executive Chairman, encourages questions and challenges from
                                                 directors in particular from INEDs who may hold different views
                                                 from the Executive Directors and Senior Management and their
                                                 comments and concerns will be closely followed up and addressed
                                                 by the Executive Directors and Senior Management.
                                                 家族企業中的獨立非執行董事一般會於企業管治方面發揮更為
                                                 積極的作用。在執行主席領導下，我們的董事會文化鼓勵董事（尤
                                                 其是獨立非執行董事）提出問題及質疑，彼等可能與執行董事及
                                                 高層管理人員持有不同觀點，而執行董事及高層管理人員將密切
                                                 跟進及應對彼等的意見及關注。

No equity-based remuneration with performance-   Director fees payable to Director, including INEDs, are fixed fees
related elements to INEDs                        without any discretionary element nor they are remunerated with
獨立非執行董事未曾授予與表現績效相關                               equity-based awards. Directors fees are also determined by the
的股本權益酬金                                          R&N Committee and the Board with reference to the roles and
                                                 responsibilities taken up by the Director and benchmarking against
                                                 peers.
                                                 應付董事（包括獨立非執行董事）的董事袍金為固定數額，並無
                                                 任何酌情因素，而酬金亦無包括以股權為基礎的獎勵。董事袍金
                                                 亦由薪酬及提名委員會與董事會參照董事所承擔的角色及職責
                                                 以及行業標準而釐定。

Company and the Director Nomination Policy. The nomination                 上刊載。凡董事會委任之董事均須於下一屆股
procedures by shareholders have been published on the website              東大會上告退。

retire at the next General Meeting.

     the Board.

     Transactions by Directors of Listed Issuers contained in Appendix            所載列之規定標準寬鬆。本公司已對全體董事

     Risk Management and Internal Control                                     風險管理及內部監控
     The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk management        董事會明白其肩負風險管理及內部監控系統之
     and internal control systems and reviewing their effectiveness.          責任，並檢討其成效。該系統之設計旨在管理
     The systems are designed to manage rather than eliminate the             而非消除無法達成業務目標之風險，並僅可為
     risk of failure to achieve business objectives, and can only provide     避免發生重大錯誤陳述或損失提供合理而非絕
     reasonable and not absolute assurance against material misstatement      對之保證。為實現此目標，董事會確保已制定
     or loss. To achieve this, the Board ensures that there is an effective   有效之風險管治架構及持續風險管理程序，以
     framework of risk governance and ongoing risk management process         達致本公司業務之長久成功。
     to promote the long-term success of the Company.

     Assessing risks and reviewing the effectiveness of internal controls is   本公司持續評估風險及檢討內部監控之成效。
     a continuing process in the Company. Our internal control system is       本公司之內部監控系統旨在合理保證：
     designed to give reasonable assurance that:

     •     Assets are prudently safeguarded;                                   •   資產獲得審慎保障；

     •     Business activities are conducted in an effective and efficient     •   以有效及高效之方式進行業務活動；
           manner;

     •     Financial reporting is accurate, timely and complete; and           •   作出準確、適時及完整之財務報告；及

     •     Laws and regulations relevant to the business are complied.         •   遵守與業務相關之法律及法規。

     Under the Company’s internal control system, management is                在本公司之內部監控系統下，管理層負責設計
     charged with the responsibility to design and implement the internal      及執行內部監控措施，而董事會及審核委員會
     controls while the Board and the Audit Committee oversee the              則監察現有內部監控系統執行之成效。
     effectiveness of the internal control system that has been put in
     place.

Risk Based Audit                                                           風險基礎審計
Using risk assessment methodology and taking into account the              集團內部審計編製由審核委員會審批的年度審
Company structure, management’s concern, nature and complexity             計計劃時採用風險評估方法，並考慮本公司架
of operation, and operating environment, Group Internal Audit              構、管理層的關注範疇、營運性質及複雜性以
prepares its annual audit plan which is reviewed and approved by           及經營環境。風險評估方法有助識別業務風險
the Audit Committee. The risk assessment methodology assists               及釐定審計之頻率。集團內部審計按照已批核
in identifying business risks and determining audit frequencies.           的審計計劃安排內部審核程序，就財務、業務
Based on the approved audit plan, Group Internal Audit schedules           及職能方面之各項營運及活動進行獨立檢討，
its internal audit programs to conduct an independent review of            並安排將其資源集中處理高風險的範疇。
different financial, business and functional operations and activities
with resources focusing on areas with higher risk.

During the FY2024/2025, Group Internal Audit issued reports to             於二零二四╱二零二五財政年度內，集團內部
Senior Management covering various operational and financial               審計向高層管理人員出具涵蓋本公司及其附屬
units of the Company and its subsidiaries. Group Internal Audit also       公司多個經營及財務單位的報告。集團內部審
conducted reviews of major projects and areas of concern identified        計亦就主要項目及管理層識別的關注範疇進行
by management.                                                             審核。


Ethics Policy and Whistleblowing System                                    道德政策及舉報制度
The Company is committed to maintain high standards of business            本公司致力在公司的所有活動及營運中保持高
ethics and corporate governance across all company’s activities and        標準的商業道德和企業管治水平。所有董事及
operations. It is the responsibility of all directors and employees to     員工均有責任遵守該等最低要求。
comply with these minimum requirement.

Risk Governance Structure                                             風險管治架構
The risk governance structure consists of three layers of roles and   風險管治架構包括下列三個層面之角色及職責：
responsibilities as explained below:

 Layer                           Roles and Responsibilities
 層面                              職務及職責
 Oversight                       Audit Committee, acting on behalf of the Board
 監督                              •     Oversees the Company’s risk management and internal control on an ongoing
                                       basis.
                                 •     Oversees those risks that warrant the Committee’s attention and supervise risk
                                       management process as part of good corporate governance.
                                 代表董事會之審核委員會
                                 •     持續監督本公司之風險管理及內部監控。
                                 •     監督委員會需要注意之該等風險並監督風險管理程序，以配合良好企業管治。


                                 Group Internal Audit
                                 •    Conducts independent review on the effectiveness of risk management and
                                      internal control system and provides reasonable assurance to Audit Committee.
                                 集團內部審計
                                 •    對風險管理及內部監控系統之成效進行獨立審閱，並向審核委員會提供合理
                                      保證。

 Risk Monitoring & Review        Enterprise Risk Management Executive Committee & Entity CEOs
 風險監察及審閱                         •     Design, implement and monitor the risk management and internal control
                                       system.
                                 •     Provide assurance to the Audit Committee on the effectiveness of risk
                                       management and internal control system.
                                 企業風險管理執行委員會及地方公司行政總裁
                                 •     設計、實施及監察風險管理及內部監控系統。
                                 •     向審核委員會提供有關風險管理及內部監控系統成效之保證。

     Risk Management Process                                                     風險管理程序
     Our risk management process is embedded in our daily operations,            我們之風險管理程序已納入日常營運範圍、財
     financial activities, compliance, strategy development & business           務 活 動、合 規、策 略 發 展 與 業 務 規 劃、投 資 決
     planning, investment decision and external factors. The processes           定及外界因素，程序如下：
     are as follows:

     •       Anticipate: Potential risks and threats that are related to         •   預 測：所 有 員 工 均 參 與 識 別 不 同 業 務 流
             different business processes and relevant to the Company’s              程及與本公司目標相關之潛在風險及威
             objectives will be anticipated and identified by all individual         脅。為 便 於 進 行 適 當 評 估，我 們 將 風 險
             staff. Risks are grouped into six clusters (including reputation,       分為六個範疇（包括信譽、策略、市場、
             strategy, market, liquidity, credit and operation) to facilitate        流動資金、信貸及營運）。
             the assessment appropriately.

     •       Rate: Each identified risk is evaluated on the basis of impact      •   評 估：按 照 董 事 會 設 定 之 風 險 偏 好，評
             and likelihood consistent with the risk appetite set by the             估 每 項 經 識 別 風 險 的 影 響 及 可 能 性。風
             Board. The risk impact and likelihood will be rated according           險 之 影 響 及 可 能 性 將 根 據 過 往 記 錄、統
             to the historical records, statistical analyses and relevant            計 分 析、相 關 經 驗 及 管 理 層 之 專 業 判 斷
             experiences, specialist and expert judgments by management.             進行評級。集團風險管理促進評估流程，
             Group Risk Management facilitates the evaluation process                包括協助企業風險管理執行委員會及審
             including the summary discussion in the Enterprise Risk                 核委員會之討論。
             Management Executive Committee and Audit Committee.

•   Respond: A delegated management is assigned to each risk           •   控 制：各 項 風 險 將 配 對 專 責 管 理 人 員，
    as risk owner to fully respond on each risk. Risk management           成 為 風 險 負 責 人，以 有 效 管 理 風 險。風
    strategies and corresponding control owners are identified by          險負責人制定風險管理策略以及指派監
    risk owner to deploy appropriate control activities.                   控負責人，實施適當監控活動。

•   Regulate: We take a close monitoring on the current status of      •   監 察：我 們 密 切 監 控 風 險 現 況。各 風 險
    risk, which is being monitored through key risk indicators with        現況的結果取決於關鍵風險指標的預設
    defined thresholds, occurrence of related incidents, control           量 度 級 別 基 準、所 發 生 之 相 關 事 故、內
    effectiveness and audit results. Any change in current status          部 監 控 程 序 之 成 效 以 及 審 計 結 果。風 險
    of risk to a worsened level will trigger a react process through       現 況 倘 出 現 任 何 不 良 轉 變，均 會 啟 動 反
    creation of a mitigation plan.                                         應程序，展開減輕風險計劃。

•   React: When there is a change on the current status of risk,       •   緩 解：當 風 險 現 況 有 所 改 變，相 應 負 責
    the corresponding owner will coordinate mitigation plan with           人 將 協 調 減 輕 風 險 計 劃 之 行 動 細 節、負
    action details, responsible person and timeline to address the         責人及時間表，以處理風險情況。
    status.

•   Report: To support the oversight of the Board on the               •   通 報：風 險 報 告 內 容 及 風 險 管 理 框 架，
    effectiveness of Vitasoy’s risk management, risk reporting             均 獲 董 事 會 批 准，協 助 董 事 會 監 察 維 他
    requirement was approved by the Board of Directors under               奶 風 險 管 理 之 成 效。集 團 風 險 管 理 定 期
    risk management framework. Periodic risk report is provided            向企業風險管理執行委員會提供風險報
    to the Enterprise Risk Management Executive Committee                  告，監 控 減 輕 風 險 計 劃 及 現 時 風 險 管 理
    summarizing the results of risk monitoring, mitigation                 實施之進度。此外，自推出管治、風險管
    plans and the current risk management implementation                   理 及 合 規 系 統（「 GRC 系 統」）以 來，企 業
    progress. Besides, since the launch of the Governance, Risk            風險管理執行委員會及風險負責人可透
    and Compliance System (“GRC System”), Enterprise Risk                  過相關系統指標獲得實時風險狀況。

Inside Information                                                      內幕消息
With respect to procedures and internal controls for the handling       有關處理及發放內幕消息之程序和內部監控方
and dissemination of inside information, the Company is aware of        面，本公司知悉，根據上市規則及《證券及期貨
the requirements under the applicable Listing Rules and Securities      條例》之適用規定以及首要原則，本公司在得悉
and Futures Ordinance and the overriding principle that inside          內幕消息後，應於切實可行情況下盡快公佈有
information should be announced as soon as reasonably practicable       關資料，除非有關資料屬於《證券及期貨條例》
after such information comes to our attention unless it falls within    中任何安全港條文所界定者則另當別論。為遵
any of the safe harbours provisions under the Securities and Futures    守監管體系下有關內幕消息披露規定，本公司
Ordinance. The Company has had a system in place with established       已有一套擁有既定政策及程序之系統。董事會
policies and procedures for complying with the Inside Information       已於二零一二年十一月二十七日採納披露合規
disclosure requirements under the regulatory regime. The Board          政策，並於二零二一年十一月二十五日進一步
has adopted a Disclosure Compliance Policy on 27th November             作出一些細微修訂。該政策乃參照證券及期貨
2012 which has been further revised with a few housekeeping             事務監察委員會所頒佈之《內幕消息披露指引》
amendments on 25th November 2021. The Policy has been                   而編製，旨在為董事及僱員提供有關評估、匯
formulated by reference to the “Guidelines on Disclosure of Inside      報及發佈內幕消息、保密及遵守股份交易限制
Information” issued by the Securities and Futures Commission.           之指引。本公司已於行為守則及員工手冊內訂
The purpose of the Policy is to provide Directors and employees         明嚴禁未經授權披露或使用公司之機密資料以
with guidelines on assessing, reporting and disseminating inside        及內幕消息。
information, maintaining confidentiality and abiding shares dealing
restrictions. The Company has included in its code of conduct and
staff handbook a strict prohibition on the unauthorised disclosure or
use of confidential and inside information.

     Dividend Policy                                                           股息政策
     The Board has adopted a Dividend Policy to provide guidance to            董事會已採納股息政策，就派發盈餘資金予股
     the Board and the management of the Company for distribution of           東向董事會及管理層提供指引。
     surplus funds to its shareholders.

     In determining the level of dividend payment, the following criteria      就決定所派付的股息水平，須計及下列準則及
     and consideration must be taken:                                          考慮因素：

     –        Sharing of success with shareholders                             －    與股東共享成果

     –        Liquidity and capital requirement                                －    流動資金及資本要求

     –        Historical trend of dividend payout                              －    過往股息派付之趨勢

     –        Benchmarking with competitors within the industry                －    與業內競爭對手之標準比較

     –        Fulfilment of financial covenants                                －    符合財務契約

     –        Taxation impact                                                  －    稅務影響

     –        Restrictions                                                     －    限制條件

     –        Other factors that may deem relevant                             －    其他視為相關之因素

     The Company intends to declare dividends to shareholders to align         本公司計劃配合扣除一次性事件影響後之股東
     with the fluctuation in profit attributable to shareholders net of one-   應佔溢利之波動向股東宣派股息，並可能除中
     off event impact and may declare special dividends from time to time      期或末期股息外不時宣派特別股息。董事會將
     in addition to interim or final dividends. The determination to pay       根據股息政策不時酌情決定是否派發股息以及
     dividends and level of dividend to be distributed will be subject to      所派發股息之水平。
     the discretion of the Board made in accordance with the Dividend
     Policy from time to time.

     executive or employee of the Company or any of its subsidiaries) to           格 參 與 者（為 本 公 司 或 其 附 屬 公 司 之 董 事、行

Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of the                          核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔的
consolidated financial statements                                        責任
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether          我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存
the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from           在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得

auditor’s report that includes our opinion. This report is made solely   我們是按照香港《公司條例》第 405 條的規定，
to you, as a body, in accordance with section 405 of the Hong Kong       僅向整體成員報告。除此以外，我們的報告不
Companies Ordinance, and for no other purpose. We do not assume          可用作其他用途。我們概不就本報告的內容，
responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person for the   對任何其他人士負責或承擔法律責任。
contents of this report.

always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements      存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤
can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,            引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能
individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected      影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴財務報表所作出
to influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of       的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。
these consolidated financial statements.

1      Material accounting policies                                        1     重大會計政策

(a)    Statement of compliance                                             (a)   遵例聲明
These financial statements have been prepared in accordance with           本財務報表是按照《香港財務報告準則會計準則》
HKFRS Accounting Standards, which collective term includes all             及香港《公司條例》之規定而編製。《香港財務報
applicable individual Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards              告 準 則 會 計 準 則》一 詞 包 括 香 港 會 計 師 公 會 頒
(“HKFRS”), Hong Kong Accounting Standards (“HKASs”) and                    佈之所有適用之個別《香港財務報告準則》、《香
Interpretations issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public      港會計準則》及詮釋。本財務報表亦符合聯交所
Accountants (“HKICPA”) and the requirements of the Hong Kong               證券上市規則（「上市規則」）之適用披露規定。
Companies Ordinance. These financial statements also comply with           本集團採納之重大會計政策於下文披露。
the applicable disclosure provisions of the Rules Governing the
Listing of Securities on the Stock Exchange (the “Listing Rules”).
Material accounting policies adopted by the Group are disclosed
below.

The HKICPA has issued certain amendments to HKFRS Accounting               香 港 會 計 師 公 會 已 頒 佈 若 干《香 港 財 務 報 告 準
Standards that are first effective or available for early adoption for     則會計準則》之修訂，並於本集團之當前會計期
the current accounting period of the Group. Note 1(c) provides             間首次生效或可供提早採納。首次應用此等適
information on any changes in accounting policies resulting from           用於本集團之新訂準則所引致於當前及過往會
initial application of these developments to the extent that they are      計期間之任何會計政策變動已於本財務報表內
relevant to the Group for the current and prior accounting periods         反映，有關資料載於附註 1(c)。
reflected in these financial statements.

(b)    Basis of preparation of the financial statements                    (b)   財務報表之編製基準
The consolidated financial statements for the year ended 31st March        截至二零二五年三月三十一日止年度之綜合財
2025 comprise the Company and its subsidiaries (together referred          務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）
to as the “Group”) and the Group’s interest in a joint venture.            以及本集團於一間合營公司之權益。

The measurement basis used in the preparation of the financial             除 附 註 1(t) 所 載 會 計 政 策 闡 釋 之 衍 生 金 融 工 具
statements is the historical cost basis except that derivative financial   按公允值計算外，編製財務報表是以歷史成本
instruments are stated at fair value as explained in the accounting        作為計量基準。
policies set out in note 1(t).

Non-current assets held for sale are stated at the lower of carrying       持作出售非流動資產按賬面值與公平值減出售
amount and fair value less costs to sell (see note 1(y)).                  成本兩者中之較低者列賬（見附註 1(y)）。

The preparation of financial statements in conformity with HKFRS           管 理 層 須 在 編 製 符 合《香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 會 計
Accounting Standards requires management to make judgements,               準則》之財務報表時作出會影響會計政策應用，
estimates and assumptions that affect the application of policies          以及資產、負債、收入及支出之報告金額之判斷、
and reported amounts of assets, liabilities, income and expenses.          估計及假設。此等估計及相關假設是根據以往
The estimates and associated assumptions are based on historical           經驗和管理層因應當時情況認為合理之多項其
experience and various other factors that are believed to be               他因素作出，其結果構成當管理層在無法依循
reasonable under the circumstances, the results of which form the          其他途徑即時得知資產與負債之賬面值時所作
basis of making the judgements about carrying values of assets and         出判斷之基礎。實際結果可能有別於估計數額。
liabilities that are not readily apparent from other sources. Actual
results may differ from these estimates.

The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an                管理層會不斷審閱各項估計和相關假設。倘若
ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in         會計估計之修訂只影響某一期間，則該修訂便
the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects only   會在估計修訂期間內確認，或如果修訂對當期
that period, or in the period of the revision and future periods if the    和未來期間均有影響，則在作出修訂之期間和
revision affects both current and future periods.                          未來期間確認。

           HKFRS 7, Financial instruments Disclosure: Supplier finance               《香港財務報告準則》第 7 號「金融工具：

     assessed to be indefinite. Any conclusion that the useful life of           不會作攤銷。無形資產之可使用年期屬無限之
     an intangible asset is indefinite is reviewed annually to determine         任何結論經每年檢討，以釐定是否存在有關事
     whether events and circumstances continue to support the indefinite         件及情況繼續支持有關資產具有無限可使用年
     useful life assessment for that asset. If they do not, the change in        期之評估。如情況有變，則會自變更日期起就
     the useful life assessment from indefinite to finite is accounted for       可使用年期從無限轉為有限之評估按未來適用
     prospectively from the date of change and in accordance with the            基準並根據上文所載就具有有限可使用年期之
     policy for amortisation of intangible assets with finite lives as set out   無形資產作出攤銷之政策會計入賬。
     above.


     (i)    Leased assets                                                        (i)   租賃資產
     At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether the                  於合約開始時，本集團會評估合約是否屬租賃
     contract is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or contains, a lease      或包含租賃。倘合約為換取代價而給予在一段
     if the contract conveys the right to control the use of an identified       時間內控制已識別資產使用之權利，則該合約
     asset for a period of time in exchange for consideration. Control is        屬租賃或包含租賃。倘客戶既有權指示已識別
     conveyed where the customer has both the right to direct the use            資產之使用，亦有權自該使用中獲得絕大部分
     of the identified asset and to obtain substantially all of the economic     經濟利益，則控制權已轉移。
     benefits from that use.

The Group recognises a loss allowance for expected credit losses               本 集 團 為 按 攤 銷 成 本 計 量 之 金 融 資 產（包 括 現
(“ECLs”) on financial assets measured at amortised cost (including             金及現金等值項目以及應收賬款及其他應收款）

Measurement of ECLs                                                            預期信貸虧損之計量
ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of credit losses. Credit              預期信貸虧損乃信貸虧損之概率加權估計。信
losses are measured as the present value of all expected cash                  貸 虧 損 以 所 有 預 期 現 金 差 額（即 根 據 合 約 應 付
shortfalls (i.e. the difference between the cash flows due to the              予本集團之現金流量與本集團預期收取之現金
Group in accordance with the contract and the cash flows that the              流量之間的差額）的現值計量。
Group expects to receive).

at initial recognition or an approximation thereof for trade and other         利率或其近似值折現。
receivables.

credit risk.

     Write-off policy                                                              撇銷政策
     The gross carrying amount of a financial asset is written off (either         倘金融資產並無實際收回前景，則其賬面總值

     (k)     Inventories                                                         (k)     存貨
     Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and net realisable value.      存貨按成本值及可變現淨值兩者中之較低者入賬。

     Cost is calculated using the weighted average method and comprises          成本值乃以加權平均方法計算，並包括所有購
     all costs of purchase, costs of conversion and other costs incurred in      貨成本、加工成本及將存貨運往其現時地點及
     bringing the inventories to their present location and condition.           達至現有狀態之其他成本。

     Net realisable value is the estimated selling price in the ordinary         可變現淨值指正常業務過程中之估計售價減去
     course of business less the estimated costs of completion and the           完成交易之估計成本及進行銷售所需之估計成本。
     estimated costs necessary to make the sale.

     When inventories are sold, the carrying amount of those inventories         出售存貨時，其賬面值於有關收入確認期內確
     is recognised as an expense in the period in which the related              認為開支。任何存貨金額撇減至可變現淨值及
     revenue is recognised. The amount of any write-down of inventories          存貨之所有虧損均於撇減或虧損之發生期內確
     to net realisable value and all losses of inventories are recognised as     認為開支。倘存貨之撇減出現任何撥回，則於
     an expense in the period the write-down or loss occurs. The amount          撥回出現期內將費用作減額確認。
     of any reversal of any write-down of inventories is recognised as a
     reduction in the amount of inventories recognised as an expense in
     the period in which the reversal occurs.

(m) Software-as-a-service (SaaS) arrangement costs                        (m) 軟件即服務安排成本
A SaaS arrangement is a service arrangement where the Group has a         軟件即服務安排指本集團有權於安排期限內使

but not control over the underlying software asset.

asset that the Group controls. Any resulting asset is recognised and      據此產生之任何資產均根據附註 1(h) 所載之無
accounted for in accordance with the policy for intangible assets as      形資產政策進行確認及入賬。不會產生資產之
set out in note 1(h). Implementation costs that do not give rise to an    實行成本於發生時計入損益，即可能於收到配
asset are recognised in profit or loss as incurred, which may be over     置 或 定 制 服 務 期 間（如 有 關 服 務 與 軟 件 即 服 務
the period the configuration or customisation services are received       不同）或於軟件即服務安排期限內（如在配置或
to the extent that such services are distinct from the SaaS, or over      定制服務方面與軟件即服務並無差異）。
the term of the SaaS arrangement to the extent the configuration or

recognised as prepayment.

     between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities for financial          債之賬面值與用於稅務目的之金額之間之暫時

            differences and it is probable that they will not reverse in the

and a reliable estimate can be made. Where the time value of                幣時間價值重大，則按預計履行責任所需開支
money is material, provisions are stated at the present value of the        之現值計提撥備。
expenditure expected to settle the obligation.

required, or the amount cannot be estimated reliably, the obligation        有關金額作出可靠之估計，便會將該責任披露
is disclosed as a contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow   為或然負債，但假如經濟利益流出之可能性渺
of economic benefits is remote. Possible obligations, whose existence       茫則除外。須視乎一宗或多宗未來事件是否發
will only be confirmed by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one           生才能確定存在與否之潛在責任，亦會披露為
or more future events are also disclosed as contingent liabilities          或然負債，但假如這類經濟利益之流出之可能
unless the probability of outflow of economic benefits is remote.           性渺茫則除外。

(aa) Segment reporting                                                       (aa) 分部報告
Operating segments, and the amounts of each segment item                     營運分部及本財務報表所呈報之各分部項目金
reported in the financial statements, are identified from the financial      額，乃根據定期向本集團高層管理人員提供之
information provided regularly to the Group’s most senior executive          財務報告中識別。高層管理人員依據該等報告
management for the purposes of allocating resources to, and                  分配資源予本集團不同業務及地域以及評估該
assessing the performance of, the Group’s various lines of business          等業務及地域之表現。
and geographical locations.

Individually material operating segments are not aggregated for              就財務報告而言，個別重大營運分部不會綜合
financial reporting purposes unless the segments have similar                呈報，除非該等分部具有類似經濟特點及在產
economic characteristics and are similar in respect of the nature of         品及服務性質、生產程序性質、客戶類型或類別、
products and services, the nature of production processes, the type          分銷產品或提供服務所採用之方式及監管環境
or class of customers, the methods used to distribute the products or        性質方面類似。倘個別而言並非屬重要之營運
provide the services, and the nature of the regulatory environment.          分部符合上述大部份特點，則可能綜合呈報。
Operating segments which are not individually material may be
aggregated if they share a majority of these criteria.

     these assets are tested for impairment whenever events or changes           36 號，凡有事件或情況變動顯示該等資產所錄

     (c)    Pillar Two income tax (continued)                                   (c)   支柱二所得稅（續）
     The Group is expected to be within the scope of the Organisation for       本集團預期將納入經濟合作與發展組織（「經合
     Economic Co-operation and Development (“OECD”) Pillar Two model            組織」）支柱二規則範本的範圍內。在本集團營
     rules. Of the jurisdictions in which the Group operates, Australia         運所在的司法管轄區中，澳洲及加拿大已頒佈
     and Canada enacted Pillar Two legislation which is effective and is        支柱二法例，其於二零二四年一月一日起的財
     applicable to the Group for the fiscal year starting on 1st January        政年度生效並對本集團適用。香港特別行政區
     2024. The governments of the Hong Kong SAR and Singapore                   及新加坡之政府預計將自二零二五年一月一日
     anticipate implementation of Pillar Two legislation starting from 1st      起實施支柱二法例。根據有關法例，於截至二
     January 2025. Under such legislation, the Group is liable to pay a         零 二 五 年 三 月 三 十 一 日 止 年 度，倘 加 拿 大（及
     top-up tax for the difference between its Global AntiBase Erosion          美 國）與 澳 洲 的 全 球 反 侵 蝕 稅 基 實 際 稅 率 低 於
     (“GloBE”) effective tax rate (“ETR”) per jurisdiction and the 15%          15%，則本集團需就其於各個司法管轄區的全
     minimum rate if the GloBE ETR of Canada (and the United States)            球反侵蝕稅基實際稅率與 15% 最低稅率之差額
     and Australia are less than 15% for the year ended 31st March              繳納補足稅。鑑於計算全球反侵蝕稅基實際稅
     2025. Given the complexities involved in calculating GloBE ETR, the        率所涉及的複雜性，本集團已就本集團經營所
     Group has undertaken a preliminary assessment of the Pillar Two            在且已頒佈支柱二法例的司法管轄區初步評估
     tax implications for the jurisdictions in which the Group operates         支柱二法例對稅項的影響。基於初步評估，本
     and have enacted Pillar Two legislation. Based on the preliminary          集 團 預 計 已 頒 佈 支 柱 二 法 例 的 加 拿 大（連 同 其
     assessment, the Group expects that Canada (with its subsidiary in          於 美 國 的 附 屬 公 司）及 澳 洲 將 可 能 符 合 過 渡 性
     United States) and Australia, where Pillar Two legislation has been        國別報告安全港標準，因此於截至二零二五年
     enacted will likely satisfy the Transitional Country-by-Country            三月三十一日止年度可能不會產生補足稅影響。
     Reporting (“CbCR”) Safe Harbour criteria and at this time it is unlikely   本集團將繼續監察支柱二法例發展，並重新評
     there will be any top-up tax impact for the year ended 31st March          估潛在稅項影響。
     2025. The Group will continue to monitor Pillar Two developments
     and reassess the potential impact on its tax position.

(d)    The fair value of investment properties (continued)                (d)   投資物業之公允值（續）
The fair value measurement is categorised as a Level 3 valuation          公允值計量根據《香港財務報告準則》第 13 號「公
under the three-level fair value hierarchy as defined in HKFRS            允值計量 」所 界 定 之 三 級 公 允 值 架 構 分 類 為 第
13, Fair value measurement . The level into which a fair value            三級估值。將某公允值計量分類之等級乃經參
measurement is classified is determined with reference to                 考如下估值方法所用數據之可觀察性及重要性
observability and significance of the inputs used in the valuation        後釐定：
technique as follows:

–      Level 1 valuations: Fair value measured using only Level 1         －     第一級估值：僅使用第一級輸入數據（即
       inputs i.e. unadjusted quoted prices in active markets for               於計量日同類資產或負債於活躍市場之
       identical assets or liabilities at the measurement date;                 未經調整報價）計量之公允值；

–      Level 2 valuations: Fair value measured using Level 2 inputs       －     第二級估值：使用第二級輸入數據（即未
       i.e. observable inputs which fail to meet Level 1, and not using         能達到第一級之可觀察輸入數據）且並未
       significant unobservable inputs. Unobservable inputs are                 使用重大不可觀察輸入數據計量之公允
       inputs for which market data are not available; and                      值。不 可 觀 察 輸 入 數 據 為 無 市 場 數 據 之
                                                                                輸入數據；及

–      Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using significant          －     第 三 級 估 值：使 用 重 大 不 可 觀 察 輸 入 數
       unobservable inputs.                                                     據計量之公允值。

The fair value of investment properties is determined by using the         投資物業之公允值乃參考可比較物業之市價使
direct comparison approach with reference to the market price of           用直接比較法釐定，並就參考交易之樓宇質素
comparable properties and adjusted for building quality and timing         及時間作出調整。公允值計量之重大不可觀察
of the reference transaction. The significant unobservable input           輸入數據乃個別物業調整率，介乎 0.95 至 1.05
in the fair value measurement is the property-specific adjusting          （二 零 二 四 年：0.94 至 1.05）。公 允 值 計 量 與 個
rate, which ranged from 0.95 to 1.05 (2024: 0.94 to 1.05). The fair       別物業調整率之間成正向關係。
value measurement is positively correlated to the property-specific
adjusting rate.

     (a)    Impairment                                                         (a)   減值
     The carrying amount of interest in joint venture was fully impaired by    截至二零二一年三月三十一日止年度，於計及

     the adverse impact of COVID-19 on future sales projections in the         通過確認減值費用 42,800,000 元，合營公司之
     year ended 31st March 2021.                                               權益之賬面值已全數減值。

     Considering the current accumulated losses and uncertainty in             考慮到當前累計虧損以及計算可收回金額時評
     evaluating market factors in the calculation of the recoverable           估市場因素的不確定性，管理層認為毋須於截
     amount, management concluded that no reversal of impairment was           至二零二五年三月三十一日止年度內作出減值
     to be made during the year ended 31st March 2025.                         撥回。

(d)    Long service payments                                              (d)   長期服務金
Hong Kong employees that have been employed continuously for              根據香港《僱傭條例》，在若干情況下，香港僱
at least five years are entitled to long service payments (“LSP”) in      員持續服務至少五年可享有長期服務金（「長期
accordance with the Hong Kong Employment Ordinance under                  服務金」）。該等情況包括僱員因嚴重不當行為
certain circumstances. These circumstances include where an               或 裁 員 以 外 之 原 因 遭 到 解 僱、僱 員 於 65 歲 或
employee is dismissed for reasons other than serious misconduct or        以上退休或僱傭合約為固定期限且屆滿後不予
redundancy, that employee resigns at the age of 65 or above, or the       續約。釐定長期服務金應付款項乃參考僱員最
employment contract is of fixed term and expires without renewal.         後薪金（上限為 22,500 元）及服務年期，並扣減
The amount of LSP payable is determined with reference to the             本集團強積金計劃（見附註 22(a)(i)）或界定供款
employee’s final salary (capped at $22,500) and the years of service,     計 劃（見 附 註 22(a)(i)）或 界 定 福 利 計 劃（見 附 註
reduced by the amount of any accrued benefits derived from the            22(c)）歸屬於本集團所作供款之任何應計權益，
Group’s contribution to the MPF scheme (see note 22(a)(i)), or the        每 名 僱 員 之 總 上 限 為 390,000 元。本 集 團 現 時
DC Plan (see note 22(a)(i)) or the DB Plan (see note 22(c)), with an      並無任何獨立資金安排以履行其長期服務金義務。
overall cap of $390,000 per employee. Currently, the Group does
not have any separate funding arrangement in place to meet its LSP
obligation.

In June 2022, the Hong Kong SAR Government (the “Government”)             於二零二二年六月，香港特別行政區政府（「政
gazetted the Hong Kong Employment and Retirement Scheme                   府」）刊 憲《2022 年 僱 傭 及 退 休 計 劃 法 例（抵 銷
Legislation (Offsetting Arrangement) (Amendment) Ordinance 2022           安排）（修訂）條例》   （「該修訂條例」），其自二零
(the “Amendment Ordinance”), which come into effect from 1st May          二五年五月一日起生效（「過渡日期」）。該修訂
2025 (the “Transition Date”). The Amendment Ordinance abolish             條例廢除僱主可利用其向強積金計劃作出之強
the statutory right of an employer to reduce its LSP payable to a         制性供款減少其應付僱員之長期服務金之法定
Hong Kong employee by drawing on its mandatory contributions to           權利。另一方面，政府於廢除後推出補貼計劃
the MPF scheme. Separately, the Government introduced a subsidy           以協助僱主。
scheme to assist employers after the abolition.

Among other things, once the abolition of the offsetting mechanism        其中，一旦取消對沖機制生效，僱主自過渡日
takes effect, an employer can no longer use any of the accrued            期 起 概 不 得 使 用 其 強 制 性 強 積 金 供 款（無 論 於
benefits derived from its mandatory MPF contributions (irrespective       過渡日期之前、當日或之後作出之供款）所產生
of the contributions made before, on or after the Transition Date)        之任何累計權益減少有關僱員服務年期之長期
to reduce the LSP in respect of an employee’s service from the            服務金。然而，倘僱員於過渡日期前已開始受僱，
Transition Date. However, where an employee’s employment                  則僱主可繼續使用上述累計權益減少截至該日
commenced before the Transition Date, the employer can continue           期前就僱員服務年期之長期服務金；另外，就
to use the above accrued benefits to reduce the LSP in respect of         過渡日期前服務年期之長期服務金將按僱員緊
the employee’s service up to that date; in addition, the LSP in respect   接過渡日期前之月薪及截至過渡日期之服務年
of the service before the Transition Date will be calculated based        期 計 算。誠 如 附 註 1(q)(ii) 所 披 露，本 集 團 已 對
on the employee’s monthly salary immediately before the Transition        抵銷機制及其取消安排進行會計處理。
Date and the years of the service up to that date. The Group has
accounted for the offsetting mechanism and its abolition as disclosed
in note 1(q)(ii).

The Group has determined that the Amendment Ordinance primarily           本集團已確定，該修訂條例主要影響本集團對
impacts the Group’s LSP liability with respect to Hong Kong               並無參與界定福利計劃之香港僱員之長期服務
employees that do not participate in the DB Plan. The Amendment           金責任。該修訂條例就本集團對已參與界定福
Ordinance has no material impact on the Group’s LSP liability with        利計劃之僱員之長期服務金責任概無重大影響。

The hedging reserve comprises the effective portion of the                 對 沖 儲 備 包 括 用 作 有 待 其 後 根 據 附 註 1(t) 所 載

in substance form part of the net investment in subsidiaries and the       生 之 外 匯 匯 兌 差 額。該 儲 備 將 根 據 附 註 1(v) 所
joint venture established outside Hong Kong. The reserve is dealt          載之會計政策處理。
with in accordance with the accounting policy set out in note 1(v).

     (g)    Capital management                                                  (g)   資本管理
     The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital are to                本集團在管理資本時之首要目標為保障本集團
     safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern, so that      能持續經營的能力，透過將產品及服務的價格
     it can continue to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for       訂於與風險相稱的水平及按合理成本籌措融資，
     other stakeholders, by pricing products and services commensurately        使其能繼續為股東提供回報及為其他利益相關
     with the level of risk and by securing access to finance at a              人士提供利益。
     reasonable cost.

     The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages its capital           本集團積極及定期對資本架構進行檢討及管理，
     structure to maintain a balance between the higher shareholder             以在較高股東回報可能附帶較高借貸水平與雄
     returns that might be possible with higher levels of borrowings and        厚資本狀況帶來之優勢及保障間取得平衡，並
     the advantages and security afforded by a sound capital position,          因應經濟環境之變化對資本架構作出調整。
     and makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of changes in
     economic conditions.

     Consistent with industry practices, the Group monitors its capital         本著業內慣例，本集團以負債對經調整資本比
     structure on the basis of a debt-to-adjusted capital ratio. For this       率作為監控其資本架構之基準。就此目的，本
     purpose, the Group defines debt as the total of bills payable, bank        集團將負債界定為應付票據、銀行貸款及租賃
     loans and lease liabilities, and adjusted capital as all components of     負債之總額，經調整資本為權益之所有組成部
     equity less unaccrued proposed dividends.                                  份減非累計擬派股息。

     The Group’s strategy, which was unchanged from the year ended              本集團之策略與截至二零二四年三月三十一日
     31st March 2024, was to maintain the debt-to-adjusted capital ratio        止年度保持不變，將負債對經調整資本比率維
     below 30%. In order to maintain or adjust the ratio, the Group may         持 於 30% 以 下。為 保 持 或 調 整 有 關 比 率，本 集
     adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders, issue new             團或會調整向股東派付之股息金額、發行新股
     shares, return capital to shareholders, raise new debt financing or sell   份、向股東退回資本、籌集新債項融資或出售
     assets to reduce debt.                                                     資產以減低負債。

     Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default on its      信貸風險指交易方違反其合約責任而導致本集

     The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable to trade and other        本集團之信貸風險主要來自應收賬款及其他應
     receivables. Management has a credit policy in place and exposure to        收款。管理層備有信貸政策，而所面臨之有關
     credit risk is monitored on an ongoing basis.                               信貸風險持續受到監控。

     The Group’s cash and bank deposits are placed with financial                本集團之現金及銀行存款乃存放於有良好信貸
     institutions with sound credit ratings, and the management consider         評級之金融機構，管理層認為本集團面臨之信
     the Group’s exposure to credit risk is low.                                 貸風險為低。

     The Group does not provide any guarantees which would expose the            本集團並無提供任何將令本集團面臨信貸風險
     Group to credit risk.                                                       之擔保。

     Trade debtors and bills receivable (which are included in trade             應收賬款及應收票據（已包括於應收賬款
     and other receivables)                                                      及其他應收款內）
     Individual credit evaluations are performed on all customers                所有要求超過特定信貸金額之客戶均須進行個

     the customer as well as pertaining to the economic environment in           收賬款及應收票據一般於發單日起計一至三個

     generally due within one to three months from the date of billing.          抵押品。

     (d)    Currency risk                                                       (d)    貨幣風險
     The Group is exposed to currency risk primarily through sales,              本集團面臨之貨幣風險主要來自因買賣及借貸

     which the transactions relate. The currencies giving rise to this risk     （「美 元」）、澳 幣（「澳 幣」）、坡 幣（「坡 幣」）、人
     are primarily United States dollars (“USD”), Australian dollars (“AUD”),    民幣（「人民幣」）及歐元（「歐元」）。
     Singapore dollars (“SGD”), Renminbi (“RMB”) and Euros (“EUR”).

     For group entities whose functional currency is Hong Kong dollars          就功能貨幣為港幣（「港幣」）之集團實體而言，
     (“HKD”), all sales and purchases are denominated in either HKD             除若干與集團實體進行之交易及採購以澳幣、
     or USD, except for certain transactions with group entities and            坡幣、人民幣及歐元計值外，所有買賣均按港

     the HKD is pegged to the USD, management does not expect that              預期有關該等以美元計值之交易並無任何重大
